7a8(1): bya ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba la spyod pa'i sems can rnams kyi lhag pa'i bsam pa dbang du 'gyur bar bya ba dang /khyod kyis bsam pa thag pa nas yongs su dris pa'i lung 
7b1(1): bstan pa'i chos nyid rab tu byung ba thob par bya ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis rlabs kyis bstan to//de nas mtshan mo'i lha mo shing thams cad kyi me tog rgyas pas 
7b2(1): bde bar gnas pas de'i tshe/don 'di nyid brnan te te bstan pa'i phyir dus gsum du byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la rnam par ltas te/tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/
7b3(1): sangs rgyas sras po khyod kyis gang dris pa/de ni bsam yas zab mo sangs rgyas yul//bsam yas zhing gi rdul phran rab snyed kyis//bskal pa mang pos des kun brjod 
7b4(1): mi nus|/sems can 'dod pa'i sems ldan snang ba dang /de bzhin gti mug mun pas bsgribs pa dang /'chab dang dregs pas bsam pa nyams gyur pas//
7b5(1): rgyal ba'i chos nyid zhi ba shes mi nus//phrag dog 'jungs pa'i dbang gi rjes 'brang ba dang /g.yo rgyus bsam pa rnyogs par gyur par dang //nyon mongs kyi sgrib pas 
7b6(1): bsgribs pa yis//sangs rgyas yul 'di shes par mi nus so//phung po khams dang skye mched gnas pa dang //'jig tshogs mang la gnas par gyur pa dang /
7b7(1): lta sems 'du shes phyin ci log gyur pas//zhi bI sa 'di shes par mi nus so/ /rgyal ba'i yul ni shes bka' zhi ba ste//rang bzhin dri med rnam par rtog pa med //
7b8(1): srid par rten cing 'khor la chags pa yis//chos 'di yang dag shes par mi nus so//sangs rgyas rigs su rigs ldan gang skyes//de bzhin gshegs 
8a1(1): pa kun gyis byin brlabs shing //chos kyi rgyal po'i gdung rabs yongs 'dzin pa//drang srong de dag yul ni 'di yin no//dkar chos 
8a2(1): rgya mtshos mi ngoms sems ldan zhing //gang rnams dge ba'i bshes kyis yongs bzung ste//thub pa'i stobs la dmigs pa'i sems sprin can//de dag 'di thos 
8a3(1): bzod pa thob par 'gyur//gang rnams bsams pa dri med rnam rtog med//bar snang mkha' bzhin phyogs dang phyogs mtshams su//mun pa rnam bsal blo gros 
8a4(1): sgron ma'i sprin//dri med de dag spyod yul 'di yin no//snying rje'i bsam pas 'gro ba'i rgya mtsho rnams//dus gsum byung ba'i gang rnams rgyas byed cing //sems 
8a5(1): can ma lus rjes 'brang byams pa can//de dag rgyal ba'i tshul 'dir 'jug par 'gyur//gang rnams 'dzin med dag pa'i sems ldan zhing //rtag par bdog pa 
8a6(1): thams cad sbyin brtsog la//sems can kun la mnyam par zhugs gyur pa//chags med de dag spyod yul sa 'di yin//nyon mongs med sems skyon med spyod 
8a7(1): ldan zhing //gang rnams 'gyod pa rnam bsal sems ldan te//sangs rgyas gdams ngag nan tan byed ldan pa//dri med de dag spyod yus 'di yin no//gang rnams 
8a8(1): mi sgul sems ldan mi skyod sems//chos kyi rang bzhin rtogs pa'i sems ldan zhing /las kyi rgya mtshor mi 'gal sems ldan pa//mi zad de dag rnam thar 'di yin 
8b1(1): no|/gang rnams mi skye'i sems dang mi ldog sems//brtson 'grus mthu dang stsal du ldan pa rnams//thams cad mkhyen tshogs brtson 'grus mtha' yas pa|
8b2(1): brtul zhugs bzang ldan de dag yul 'di yin//zhi ba'i sems ldan mnyam par gzhag pa gang //shin du zhir gyur rims dang bral ba rnams//kun mkhyen    
8b3(1): bsam gtan yan lag mtshor spyod pa//rab tu zhi ba de dag tshul 'di yin//gang rnams chags pa kun las sems grol zhing /chos kyi rang bzhin khong du chud   
8b4(1): sems can//rgyal ba'i (chos )dbyings rtogs par khong chud pa//shes rab sgron ma de dag tshul 'di yin/sems can rang bzhin rtogasems ldan gyur cing //
8b5(1): gang rnams srid pa'i mtsho la chags med sems//sems can sems la gzugs brnyan zla 'char ba//lam mkhan de dag rnam thar 'di yin no//dus 
8b6(1): gsum byung ba'i rgyal ba rgya mtsho rnams//smon lam gang chen mtsho'i rgyud skyes pa'i//zhing rnams kun du phyi ma'i mthar spyod pa//kun du bzang po de dag tshul 
8b7(1): 'di yin//gang rnams chos dbyings tshul rnams rgya mtsho yis//'gro ba rgya mtsho 'du dang 'jig pa yi//bskal bar bcas pa khong du chud pa rnams//
8b8(1): rtog med de dag rnam thar 'di yin na//zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran thams cad la//sangs rgyas grangs med shing rgyal drung bzhugs shing //byang chub 'tshang 
9a1(1): @#/ O /rgya 'gro 'dul mthong ba yi//chags med mig can de dag tshul 'di yin//khyod ni skal pa rgya mtsho rgya chen du//dge ba'i bshes gnyen mang po bsnyen bskur 
9a2(1): te|/chos 'dod chos tshol mi skyo 'dir phyin pas//thos nas de ni yongs su gzung bar nus//khyod kyis bsam pa yongs su sbyang ba'i phyir//bde gshegs byin 
9a3(1): brlabs bsam gyis mi khyab pas//rnam par snang mdzad tshad med yul 'di yang //kho mo'i tshig las chags(pa) med par 'byung //rigs kyi bu sngon 'das pa'i dus bskal 
9a4(1): ba 'jig rten gyi kham+sa kyi rdul shin du phra ba snyed kyi pha rol de'i (yang) pha snga zhig tu 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gser rin po che'i ri'i spos rnam pa(ra) snang ba zhes bya ba byung ste/rigs kyi bu 'jig rten gyi 
9a5(1): khams rgya mtsho gser rin po( ) che'i ri spos rnam par snang ba der de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes ri bo'i 'od chos kyi dbyings su snang ba'i gzi brjid rgyal po zhes bya ba byung ste/de bzhin gshegs 
9a6(1): pa ye shes kyi ri bo'i 'od chos kyi dbying su snang ba'i gzi brjid ( )rgyal po de sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mdzad pa'i tshe/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshogaser rin po che'i ri spos 
9a7(1): rnam par snang ba zhes bya ba/'jig rten gyi khams rgya( )mtsho'i sa dang ri rdul shin du phra ba snyedakyi 'jig rten gyi khams 'jam klas pa bstan du yod pa dang ldan pa/'jig rten gyi khams 
9b1(1): kyi rgyud re re zhing yang /bskal pa'i grangs shin bstan pa/'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul shin du phra ba snyed dang /ldan pa'i bskal pa re re zhing yang bskal pa bar ma'i shin du stan pa du ma 
9b2(1): ma dang ldan pa/bskal pa bar ma re re zhing yang 'jig rten gyi khams rnam pa thams cad du bya ba bstan pa du ma dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba dang /rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs su 
9b3(1): stan du yod pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'abyung ba ba re re zhing yang mdo sde yang dag par bstan pa/'jig rten gyi lams kyi rdul shin du phra bsnyed brjod du yod pa/mdo sde re re la 'ang byang chub
9b4(1): sems dpa'i lung bstan pa 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i rdul shin du phra ba snyed stan du yod pa/sems can 'dul ba mtha' dang dbung med pa shin du bstan pa theg pa tha 
9b5(1): dad pa'i tshul gyis bsgrubs pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa dang /cho 'phrul sna tshogs kyis rnam par 'dul ba dang ldan pa yongs su sbyangs te/rigs kyi bu 'jig rten gyi khams 
9b6(1): rgya mtsho gser rin po che'i ri spo rnam par snang ba der 'jig rten gyi khams bar ma'i rgyud phyogs kun du mngon par ltas pa'i sgo rgyal mtshan gyis rnam par brgyan pa zhes bya ba byung ste//
9b7(1): rigs kyi bu 'jig rten gyi lams bar ma'i rgyud phyogs kum du sgo mngon par ltas pa rgyal mtshan gyis rnam par rgyan pa der 'jig rten gyi khams
10a1(1): @#/ O /rin po che thams cad kyi mdog kun du snang ba'i dpal zhes bya ba 'jig gyi rten gyi khams kyi gzhi dang /mtshanas kyi rgyan rin po che'i rgyal po de bzhin gshegs
10a2(1): pa thams cad kyi snying po byang chub rab tu snang ba las byas pa/rin po che sna tshogs kyis me tog rgya mtsho la gnas pa/lhun rin po che'i rgyal po de gzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku sprul 
10a3(1): pa'i gzugs brnyan rab tu ston pa las bsgrubs pa/lha'i grong khyer gyi dbyibs su 'dug pa rnam par dag cing kun nas nyon mongs pa byung bar gyur te/'jig rten gyi khams der  
10a4(1): gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams ri rab kyi rdul shin du phra ba snyed byung bar gyur to//gling bzhi pa'i ri rab kyi rdul shin du pra ba snyed pa de dag gi gling bzhi pa dbu ma rin po che sna tshog+sa
10a5(1): kyi rtse mo'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba byung ste/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyikhams rin po sna tshogs kyi rtse mo'i rgyal mtshan der gling chu zheng du dag chad brgya phrag stong yod pa zhigi
10a6(1): byung na|gling re rer yang grong khyer chen po stong phrag re re byung ngo //gling chen po bzhi pa de'i 'dzam bu'i gling gi dbus su /rgyal po'i gnas rin po che'i sa las rnam par brgyan
10a7(1): pa sprin gyi sgron ma zhes bya ba grong khyen stong phrag bcu'i 'khor dang ldan pa byung ngo//de'i tshe 'dzam bu'i gling de'i mi rnams kyang tshe'i tshad lo stong phrag bcu thub par gyur to//
10a8(1): rgyal po'i gnas rin po che 'i sa las rnam par brgyan pa sprin gyi sgron ma der/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po chos thams cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi dgyil 'khor rab tu sgrog pa 
10b1(1): zhes bya ba byung ste/'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyal po chos thams cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi dkyil 'khor ba du sgrog pa de'i blon po ni lnga brgya byung ngo //bcun mo ni stong phrag drug
10b2(1): byung ngo //sras ni kun kyang dpa' bo rdul phod pa/yan lag dam pa'i gzugs dang ldan pa/blta na sdug pa/gzi brjid che ba/stobs che ba brgya phrag bdun byung ngo //
10b3(1): rgyal po chos thams cad kyi da ro'i gdugs kyi dkyil 'khod rab tu sgrog pa de'i tshe la/'dzam bu'i gling thams cad gdugs gcig gis skyabs par gyur te/pha rol gyi 'khor dang 
10b4(1): dgra thams cad kyang shin tu choms par gyur ro//de'i tshe 'jig rten gyi khams der bskal pa bar ma ni zad du cha/rnyog pa lnga ni 'jig rten du byung /dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam 
10b5(1): ni nub par gyur nas/sems can mi dge bcu'i lam la spyod pa/phal cher ngan song du 'ang 'dod par gyur /de dag mi dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam/yongs su 'dzin pa'i rgyus tshe 
10b6(1): thung ba dang /longs spyod chung ba dang /gzugs ma legs pa dang /kha dog mi sdug pa dang /lus ma legs pa dang /bde ba'i rnam pa nyung ba dang /sdug bsngal nyams 
10b7(1): su myong ba'i rnam par mang ba dang /phan tshun spu zhing 'drid pa'i rang bzhin can/phan tshun dbye ba la zhugs pa dang /zhe gcod pa'i tshig la spyod pa dang /'chal pa'i tshig 
10b8(1): du smra ba dang /'dod pa mi zad pas zil gyis gnon pa dang /zhe sdang ba'i yid kyis rtog pa dang ldan pa dang /lta ba'i thibs po sna tshogs kyi mya ngan du khyams par gyur 
10b9(1): nas/de dag chos ma yin pa'i 'dod chags la zhen cing /'dod pa mi zad pa sa zil gyis non la/log pa'i chos kyis kun nas 'khor bas sa shing rtsi dang /lo 
11a1(1): @#/ O /zas skye bar 'gyur ba'i char yang dus su mi 'bab par 'gyur te/sems can de dag kyang rtswa dang gol ba dang /sman dang nags tshal dang /bza shing gi ra ba dang //
11a2(1): sdong po dag kyang skams par gyur nas/nad sna tshogs kyis thebs shing /phyogs dang phyogs mtshams su skyabs phan tshun 'khod mi myong zhing g.yen spro bar gyur te/de dag kun kyang gcig tu
11a3(1): 'dus nas rgyal po'i gnas rin po che'i sa las rnam par brgyan pa/sprin gyi sgron ma gdan ba der dong ste/kun nas bskor nas la la ni lag pa gyen du sgreng /la la ni thal mo sbyor /la la ni

11a5(1): ram mkha'i dbyings su g.yo ba/la la ni gos med cing sgren mor 'khod/la la ni kha mig mi sdug par gyur cing /rgyal po chos thams cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi dkyil 'khor rab tu

11a8(1): srog chad la thug go/'chi bar gyur ma khad do//zhes smre sngags sna tshogs su blo zhing dbyangs kyi rnam pa sna tshogs dang /tshig sna tshogs kyis gdong mi sdug par gyur bar 'du shes dang /
11b1(1): tshig sna tshogs kyi tha snyad dang /cho 'bring dang /skad kyi gdangs don sna tshogs brjod pa'i tshig dang /ngag gi sa 'o dod bos so//rgyal po'i gnas de'i skyes pa dang /bud med dang /khye'u dang ;
11b2(1): bu mo bkres pa dang /skom pas mnar ba/lus la rgyan med pa/sgren mo gos med pa/kha dog dang mdog mi sdug pa/lus gas shing rtsub pa/sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde la mi 
11b3(1): dga' ba de dag thams cad kyang bde ba ni mngon par 'dod /sdug bsngal gyi 'jigs pas ni brla khyer nas/rgyal po shes pa'i skyes bu chen po chos thams cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi dkyil
11b4(1): 'khor rab tu sgrog pa de la bde ba thams cad thob par bya ba'i 'du shes dang /sdug bsngal thams cad dang 'grogs par bya ba'i 'du shes dang/'tsho ba'i 'du bsam pa dang ldan zhing gtor 
11b5(1): thob par byi ba'i 'du shes dang /mu stegs thong bar bya ba'i 'du shes dang lam chen po la nan tan tu bya ba'i 'du shes dang /gru chen po'i 'du shes dang /ye shes kyi gling chen po'i 'du shes 
11b6(1): don chen po rab tu thob par bya ba'i 'du shesadang /mtho ris kyi dga' ba dang /bde ba thams cad rab tu thob par bya ba'i bdu shes kyi sa mgon dang skyabs tshol bar gyur to//rgyal po chos thams cad kyi
11b7(1): nga ro'i gdugs kyi dkyil 'khor rab tu sgrog pas 'khor kun nas slong ba mang po snying rje rje skad du 'bod pa'i 'o dod chen po de thos nas/de snying rje chen po'i sgo brgya stong grangs med pa phrag bcu la zhugs
11b8(1): par gyur te//de snying rje chen po'i tshul yid la bab pa'i sems su gyur nas/yud tsam zhig rtse gcig tu bsams te/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa'i tshig bcu brjod do//bcu gang zhe na/kyi hud kyi sems
12a1(1): @#/ O /can 'di dag ni 'khor ba'i g.yang sa chen por lhung skyabs med pa lta na/nam zhig 'khor ba'i g.yang sa chen pir ltung ba'i sems can rnams/de bzhin
12a2(1): gshegs pa'i sa la dgod pa gnas su 'gyur //kyi hud kyi sems can 'di dag nyon mongs pa'i gnod pas gtses shing skyabs med pa lta na/nan zhig bdag sems can nyon mongs
12a3(1): pa sna tshogs kyi 'jigs pa dang /gnod pas gtses te/skyabs med pa rnams kha na ma thob med pa'i    las kyi mtha' la 'god pas skyabs su 'gyur/kyi hud sems can
12a4(1): 'di dag 'jig rten na rga shi' 'jigs pas tshar cod cing mgon med pa lta na/nam zhig bdag sems can mgon med pa rnams kyi 'khor ba'i 'jigs pa thams cad rnam par sel
12a5(1): bas mgon du 'gyur//kyi hud sems can 'di dag 'jig rten gyi 'jigs pa sna tshogs kyis gtses te/rten med pa lta na/rnam zhig bdag sems can gyi 'jigs pa sna tshog+sa
12a6(1): kyis gtses pa rnams shin du bde zhing skyon med pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i lam la 'god pas rten du 'gyur /kyi hud kye ma sems can gyi 'jig rten ni ma rig pa'i mun par    
12a7(1): 'thoms te/the tsom dang yid gnyis kyi rab rib kyis sgribs pa lta na/nam zhig bdagasems can thams cad kyi ma rig pa'i mun pa rnam par sel bas sgron mar gyur par 'ong /
12a8(1): kyi hud sems can 'di dang ni snang ba dang bral ba lta na/nam zhig bdag ye shes kyi sgo rab rib med pa yongs su ston pas ye shes chen po'i snang ba 'byin par 'gyur //kyi hud
12b1(1): kye sems can 'di dag ni phrag dog dang /'jungs pa dang /g.yo dang /sgyu'i rnyog pas bsam pa rnyog pa can du gyur te/ye shes kyi mdog gsal ba med pa lta na/nam zhig
12b2(1): bdag sems can thams cad shin du dag pa la rab tu 'god cing bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi mdog rab tu gsal bar byed par 'gyur /kyi hud kyi sems can gyi 'jig rten ni
12b3(1): 'khor ba rgya mtsho'i yul gyi rgyud du zhugs shing 'dren pa med pa lta na/nam zhig bdag sems can thams can chos rgya mtsho'i tshul la 'dzud cing 'dren par 'gyur par 'ong /
12b4(1): kyi hud kye sems can gyi 'jig rten ma dul zhib rnam par 'dren pa med pa lta na/nam zhig bdag sems canathams cad rnam pa thams cad du yongs su smin par bya ba dang /rnam par
12b5(1): 'dul ba la de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis dus las mi 'da' zhing rnam par 'dren par gyur par 'ong ngo //kyi hud kyi sems can gyis 'jig rten ni dmus long du gyur te/
12b6(1): yongs su 'dren pa med pa lta na//nam zhig bdag sems cad thams cad sgrib pa med pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi tshul la 'dzud cing yongs su 'dren par 'gyur /de skad
12b7(1): du de snying rje chen po dang ldan pa'i tshig rnam pa bcu brjod nas/rgyal po'i gnas der dril chen po brdungs te/gtong ba chen po'i rnga bo che yong yongs su brdungs nas/su ci 'dod 
12b8(1): pa de la de sbyin gyis/'gro ba thams cad kyang yongs su tshim par bya'o zhes sgra brjod de/des 'dzam bu'i gling thams cad kyi rgyal po'i gnas thams cad dang /grong dang 
16a1(1): @#/ O /chen rin po che'i chun po'i lda ldi la rgyus pa mngon par spyangs pa/nor bu rin po che'i dam pa'i phreng ba kun nas mngon par spyangs pa'i rgyan dang ldan pa/
16a2(1): lha rdzas kyi sgra yid du 'ong pa'i gdangs grags par byung ba sems can thams cad dge ba'i las kyi lam la skul ba'i dril gyi sgra rnam par grag par byung bar 'gyur te/rgyal po chos thams
16a3(1): cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi dkyi 'khor rab tu sgrog pa de/rin po che'i rdul yab kyis mngon par g.yab cing /lha'i dbang po brgya byin pas kyang lhag par gzi brjid rab tu 'bar
16a4(1): bas shin tu mdzes so//de seng ge'i khri de la 'dug ma thag tu 'gro ba mang po de dag thams cad mngon du gyur te/thal mo sbyar nas rgyal po de la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas rgyal po chos 
16a5(1): thams cad kyi nga ro'i gdugs kyi bkyil 'khor rab tu sgrog pa/de'i drung du slong ba srog chags bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong sna tshogs dngos po sna tshogs yongs su len pa/yobyad sna tshog+sa
16a6(1): la mos ba/rigs sna tshogs dang ldan pa/'gro ba'i rgyud sna tshogs su gtogs pa/'dod pa sna tshogs kyi sems dang ldan pa/smon pa dang bsam pa sna tshogs can/phyogs sna
16a7(1): tshogs nas 'dus pa/yul gyi spyod pa sna tshogs la gnas pa/longs spyod sna tshogs la mngon par smon pa'i sems dang ldan pa/bsam pa dang yongs su rtogs pa sna tshogs 
16a8(1): can;mi'i ris sna tshogs su gtogs pa/rigs kyi skye gnas sna tshogs su skyes pa/yul sna tshogs nas 'ongs pa/sgra skad dang tshig gi 'du byed sna tshogs dang ldan pa /
16b1(1): dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs 'byin pa/dngos po sna tshogs kyi don du gsol ba 'debs pa tshig dang ngag sna tshogs brjod pa/bsod nams kyi ri rab chen po de 'ba' zhig
16b2(1): zhig gi gdong du lta ba 'di 'ba' zhig shes pa'i skyes bu chen po yin zhes nges par sems pa bsod nams chen pos bstan pa'o//skyes bu chen po'i zla ba'o//gtong ba chen po'i 
16b3(1): bsam pa dang ldan pa'o//zhes bya bar 'dun pa'i sems kyis rtog pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam gyi sems kyis 'dus pa/byang chub sems dpa'i smon 'am gyi sems
16b4(1): kyis sprul pa'i slong mo blo'i tshogs chen po de mthong nas/slongs mo ba de dag la sems dga' ba dang /sems mos pa dang /sems spro ba dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen mthong

56a3(1): bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas thams cad la sku slu ba med pa'i sems dang/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug par sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /'dul ba rgyun mchid pa'i phyir thams cad 
56a4(1): mkhyen pa nyid du smon lam chen po 'debs pa'i sems dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag gi tshogs sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin du phra ba snyed kyis yongs su rdzogs pa'i

56a7(1): pa'abyung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi tshul rgya mtsho yod de chog nges par rtogs pas mngon du bya ba'i    sems dang ldan pa thams 
56a8(1): cad mkhyen pa'i lam gyi rnam pa yongs su rdzogs par bya bas mngon du bya bar gcig tu sems dang ldan pa kha na ma tho med pa'i las yang dag par spyad par sems pa'i sems dang ldan pa//
56b1(1): byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su dag par bya bas mngon du bya ba'i sems dang ldan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan thams cad la nan tan du bya b+sa 
56b2(1): yongs su grub par sems dang ldan pa/byang cub sems dpa'i lam gyi rgyan thams cad mngon par bsgrub pa'i sems dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du (de)dmigs pa'i tshogs rgya

56b4(1): la rab tu zhugs pas /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa kun du bzang po mngon par bsgrub pa'i sem dang ldan pa/yongs su spyad pa'i tshul thams cad la rab tu zhugs pa ni byang chub sem+sa 
56b5(1): dpa'i yon tan yongs su rdzogs par bya ba la dngos po thams cad rnam par sgom pas dngos po med pa'i tshul la zhugs pa'i sems dang ldan pa/yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems 
56b6(1): dpa'i skye ba gsum pa chos kyi tshul la nges par rtogs pas gzhag pa'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon du gyur pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba bzhi pa 
56b7(1): dus gsum snang bar byed pa'i lhag pa'i bsam pa rnam par dag pa'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' lhag pa'i bsam pa'i dbyings la gya non 
56b8(1): par yongs su dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la snang ba rab tu thob pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i tshul rgya mtsho la rab tu zhugs pa yin/bsam pa gtan pa rdo rje'i kham+sa 
57a1(1): @#//lta bus yongs su zin pa'i sems dang ldan pas shin du brtan pa yin/srid pa'i lam gyi rgyud du skye ba las phyin phyogs su phyogs pa yin/de bzhin gshegs

57a8(1): mtha' yas pas mtha' las rgal bar rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la bzod pa'i snang ba rab tu thob pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la gnas pa'i bzod pa dang ldan

57b5(1): po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skyo ba drug pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/

57b7(1): 'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi smon lam chen po la rnam par dag pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba la rang bzhin 
57b8(1): gcig pa'i dge ba'i rtsa bar gyur ba yin/sangs rgyas thams cad dang lus gcig dung gyur pa yin /chos dkar pos 'jig rten gzhan du 'gro ba yin/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs
